- **person-identify**: Look up the current user's identity (real name, company, job title, department, email, employee ID, manager, and direct reports) from the corporate directory through LDAP. The bind uses the existing Kerberos ticket. Captures the user's org edges — manager and direct reports — so the KB can resolve who "our team" is. Use to establish who the knowledge base belongs to, when CLAUDE.md needs the user's identity, or when the user asks "who am I" / for their own directory record. To look up someone *other* than the current user, use the sibling `person-lookup` skill instead. - **persona-design**: 当需要为 AI 产品定义核心身份、角色声明和能力边界时调用此 skill。典型场景包括：设计新 AI 产品的 system prompt 首段、为不同场景创建差异化角色（如教学助手 vs 编程代理）、重新定义 AI 与用户的关系框架。 不适用于：纯人格风格调优（应使用 personality-system）、安全规则制定（应使用 safety-guardrails）、工具集成（应使用 tool-specification）。 关键 trigger 信号：产品需要 AI 有明确自我认知、多个角色共享底层能力但身份不同、需要限制 AI 在特定领域内运作、用户会问"你是谁"。 ([kangarooking/system-prompt-skills](https://github.com/kangarooking/system-prompt-skills/tree/HEAD/persona-design) · ★193)

- **personality**: Personality mode switcher. Shapes all responses in a chosen character voice while keeping full technical accuracy. Modes: it-helpdesk, corporate-buzzword, stackoverflow, pa-pm, intern, linkedin-bro, legal-counsel, sysadmin-80s, phb, australian, elon, altman, stoner, linus, stallman, ballmer, snoop, tony-robbins, oprah, scrum-master, leghorn, vader, ltt, zoomer. Use when user invokes /personality &lt;mode&gt;, or says "talk like X", "switch to X mode". Say "normal mode" or "stop personality" to deactivate. - **personality**: The tone layer for every Claude Cat behavior. Defines three levels (minimal, playful, full-cat) read from config.json that shape vocabulary and formatting of user-facing output without ever changing the technical content. Use when generating any status message, nudge, report, or recovery prose from a cat behavior, or when the user says "change the cat's tone", "make it less/more playful", "switch to minimal/full-cat".
